I have to roll over onto my side.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Describing a physical change of position or state.","So as to reverse up/down orientation, or otherwise change orientation by rotating."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Swap those cables over. The red one should be in the left socket.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Describing a physical change of position or state.","So as to reverse or exchange position(s)."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He came over to our way of thinking on the new project.","type":"example"},{"text":"I switched over to Channel 9.","type":"example"},{"text":"I signed the property over to him.","type":"example"},{"text":"This advertisement doesn't clearly get the idea over.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Expressing figurative movement from one position or state across to another."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I'll see you over at the club.","type":"example"},{"text":"Look over there!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Indicating a direction or location away from the speaker, usually roughly horizontally or visualised as such."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Let's talk over the project at tomorrow's meeting.","type":"example"},{"text":"Let me think that over.","type":"example"},{"text":"I'm going to look over our department's expenses.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Thoroughly;
